    print on/off         USB+BT data stream on/off

    go N                 forward N sec (1~60)
    back N               backward N sec (1~60)
    spin left N          spin in place left N sec (1~60)
    spin right N         spin in place right N sec (1~60)
    stop                 stop motor immediately (also cancels track)
    track on/off         start/stop autonomous line tracking (log: RAM-only during run until buffer nearly full, backed up to flash on off)
    speed N              speed level (1~40, default 12, until changed)
    turnspeed N          outer wheel ratio on sharp/hairpin turn (0~100%, default 65)
    mediumratio N        inner wheel speed on medium turn CH3/CH6 (0~100%, default 35)
    sharpratio N         inner wheel reverse ratio on sharp turn CH2/CH7 (0~100%, default 30)
    xsharpratio N        inner wheel reverse ratio on hairpin turn CH1/CH8 (0~100%, default 60)
    mediumspeed N        [bangbang] forward speed on medium turn CH3/CH6 (0~100%, default 85)
    sharpspeed N         [bangbang] forward speed on sharp turn CH2/CH7 (0~100%, default 65)
    hairpinspeed N       [bangbang] forward speed on hairpin turn CH1/CH8 (0~100%, default 50)
    minpwm N             [bangbang] outer wheel min forward PWM, anti-stall floor (0~255, default 70)
  -- algorithm manager (named param profiles, base=bangbang/pid) --
    algolist             list algo profiles (id name base params, *=active)
    algouse N            switch active profile to id N
    algonew BB|PID name  create a new profile (default params)
    algocopy N name      duplicate profile N to a new slot
    algoname N name      rename profile N
    algodel N            delete profile N (not active/last)
    algo 0/1             alias: switch to id 0(bangbang)/1(pid) default profile
    pid kp N             PID proportional gain (float, default 40.0)
    pid ki N             PID integral gain (float, default 0.0)
    pid kd N             PID derivative gain (float, default 5.0)
    slewrate N           max PWM change per second (float, default 800, smaller = smoother/less zigzag)
    lograte N            RAM log sample interval ms (default 10; 1=near per-loop/~1lap, 500=save RAM)
    whiteref CH VALUE    set CHx (1~8) white reference (analog weighting for PID), memory only
    blackref CH VALUE    set CHx (1~8) black reference (analog weighting for PID), memory only
    save                 save speed+turnspeed+mediumratio+sharpratio+xsharpratio+medium/sharp/hairpin speed+minpwm+algo+pid gains+slewrate+file log on/off+thresholds+white/black refs to flash (/config.json)
    config               print current config as JSON
    mem                  show free heap + ram log buffer size (auto-sized at boot, see ram_log_auto_init())
    log dump             print current RAM log buffer over Serial (most recent track on run, no flash involved)
    log clear            wipe all /track.log.* segments, #ID resets to 0
    log status           show whether file log is on, capacity, next #ID, wrapped y/n
    log format           print event/heartbeat line format + modeCode legend
    threshold CH VALUE   set CHx (1~8) threshold, memory only
    help                 show this help

>>> log line format: E<dt> <patHex> <modeCode> <L> <R>[ <err>]  (H=heartbeat instead of E)
>>>   E行dt=ms since previous mode switch (not reset by heartbeats in between)
>>>   H行dt=ms since previous logged line (heartbeat cadence)
>>>   patHex=2-digit hex bitmask (bit7=CH8..bit0=CH1, 1=white); L/R=actual PWM sent; err=PID error (PID mode only)
>>> modeCode: 0=STRAIGHT 1/2=unused(旧mild档已移除) 3=MEDIUM_L 4=MEDIUM_R 5=SHARP_L 6=SHARP_R
>>>           7=HAIRPIN_L 8=HAIRPIN_R 9=CROSS A=LOST_L B=LOST_R C=LOST_STOP P=PID
>>> markers: >>> TRACK_ON t=<ms> / >>> PARAMS ... / >>> TRACK_OFF t=<ms> elapsed=<ms>ms duration=<ms>ms lines=<n>
>>>   elapsed=track on~off挂钟时长, duration=事件/心跳数据实际覆盖的时长(缓冲区提前写满时会比elapsed短)
